+LocalMatrix distributeMatrix(const Configuration& configuration, const offset_t nonZeros,
+                             std::vector<double>& vector, std::vector<double>& reference,
+                             const int ranks) {
+    const index_t firstRow = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, g_rank, ranks);
+    const index_t lastRow = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, g_rank + 1, ranks);
+    LocalMatrix local;
+    local.rows = lastRow - firstRow;
+
+    if (g_rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<double> globalValues(static_cast<size_t>(nonZeros));
+        std::vector<index_t> globalColumns(static_cast<size_t>(nonZeros));
+        std::vector<offset_t> globalRowOffsets(static_cast<size_t>(configuration.rows) + 1);
+
+        fill(vector.data(), configuration.rows, configuration.maxValue);
+        fill(globalValues.data(), nonZeros, configuration.maxValue);
+        initRandomMatrix(globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(), nonZeros, configuration.rows);
+
+        if (configuration.validate != 0) {
+            reference.resize(configuration.rows);
+            spmvCpuOpenMP(globalValues.data(), globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(),
+                           vector.data(), configuration.rows, reference.data());
+        }
+
+        for (int destination = 0; destination < ranks; ++destination) {
+            const index_t destinationFirst = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination, ranks);
+            const index_t destinationLast = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination + 1, ranks);
+            const offset_t firstEntry = globalRowOffsets[destinationFirst];
+            const offset_t entryCount = globalRowOffsets[destinationLast] - firstEntry;
+            const size_t rowOffsetCount = static_cast<size_t>(destinationLast - destinationFirst) + 1;
+
+            if (destination == 0) {
+                local.values.assign(globalValues.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry),
+                                    globalValues.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry + entryCount));
+                local.columns.assign(globalColumns.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry),
+                                     globalColumns.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry + entryCount));
+                local.rowOffsets.assign(
+                    globalRowOffsets.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(destinationFirst),
+                    globalRowOffsets.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(destinationLast) + 1);
+            } else {
+                mpiSendLarge(globalRowOffsets.data() + destinationFirst, rowOffsetCount, MPI_UINT64_T,
+                             destination, 100);
+                if (entryCount != 0) {
+                    mpiSendLarge(globalValues.data() + firstEntry, static_cast<size_t>(entryCount), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                                 destination, 101);
+                    mpiSendLarge(globalColumns.data() + firstEntry, static_cast<size_t>(entryCount), MPI_UINT32_T,
+                                 destination, 102);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        local.rowOffsets.resize(static_cast<size_t>(local.rows) + 1);
+        mpiReceiveLarge(local.rowOffsets.data(), local.rowOffsets.size(), MPI_UINT64_T, 0, 100);
+        const offset_t entryCount = local.rowOffsets.back() - local.rowOffsets.front();
+        local.values.resize(static_cast<size_t>(entryCount));
+        local.columns.resize(static_cast<size_t>(entryCount));
+        mpiReceiveLarge(local.values.data(), local.values.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 101);
+        mpiReceiveLarge(local.columns.data(), local.columns.size(), MPI_UINT32_T, 0, 102);
+    }
+
+    mpiBroadcastLarge(vector.data(), vector.size(), MPI_DOUBLE);
+
+    const offset_t firstEntry = local.rowOffsets.front();
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int64_t row = 0; row < static_cast<int64_t>(local.rowOffsets.size()); ++row) {
+        local.rowOffsets[static_cast<size_t>(row)] -= firstEntry;
+    }
+    return local;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+T* allocateDevice(const size_t count) {
+    T* pointer = nullptr;
+    const size_t allocationCount = std::max<size_t>(count, 1);
+    if (allocationCount > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+        abortWithCuda(cudaErrorMemoryAllocation, "device allocation size check", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&pointer, allocationCount * sizeof(T)));
+    return pointer;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ columns,
+                               const offset_t* __restrict__ rowOffsets,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector, double* __restrict__ output,
+                               const index_t rows) {
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const unsigned int warp = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;
+    const unsigned int row = blockIdx.x * WARPS_PER_BLOCK + warp;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const offset_t begin = rowOffsets[row];
+    const offset_t end = rowOffsets[row + 1];
+    for (offset_t entry = begin + lane; entry < end; entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += values[entry] * vector[columns[entry]];
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        output[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+int selectLocalGpu() {
+    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank, MPI_INFO_NULL,
+                                  &localCommunicator));
+    int localRank = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank));
+
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        abortWithCuda(cudaErrorNoDevice, "cudaGetDeviceCount", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+    }
+    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator));
+    return device;
+}

The multi-rank validation path also passes (including ranks with no assigned rows). I found and fixed one zero-NNZ transfer edge case before the final rebuild; it only affected empty matrix partitions.

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithMpi(const char* operation, const char* file, const int line,

+    int errorLength = 0;

+    std::abort();

+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int destination,
+                  const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t sent = 0;
+    while (sent < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - sent));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(data + sent, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        sent += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiReceiveLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int source,
+                     const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t received = 0;
+    while (received < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - received));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(data + received, chunk, datatype, source, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                           MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));
+        received += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBroadcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t broadcast = 0;
+    while (broadcast < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - broadcast));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(data + broadcast, chunk, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        broadcast += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}

+LocalMatrix distributeMatrix(const Configuration& configuration, const offset_t nonZeros,
+                             std::vector<double>& vector, std::vector<double>& reference,
+                             const int ranks) {
+    const index_t firstRow = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, g_rank, ranks);
+    const index_t lastRow = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, g_rank + 1, ranks);
+    LocalMatrix local;
+    local.rows = lastRow - firstRow;
+
+    if (g_rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<double> globalValues(static_cast<size_t>(nonZeros));
+        std::vector<index_t> globalColumns(static_cast<size_t>(nonZeros));
+        std::vector<offset_t> globalRowOffsets(static_cast<size_t>(configuration.rows) + 1);
+
+        fill(vector.data(), configuration.rows, configuration.maxValue);
+        fill(globalValues.data(), nonZeros, configuration.maxValue);
+        initRandomMatrix(globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(), nonZeros, configuration.rows);
+
+        if (configuration.validate != 0) {
+            reference.resize(configuration.rows);
+            spmvCpuOpenMP(globalValues.data(), globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(),
+                           vector.data(), configuration.rows, reference.data());
+        }
+
+        for (int destination = 0; destination < ranks; ++destination) {
+            const index_t destinationFirst = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination, ranks);
+            const index_t destinationLast = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination + 1, ranks);
+            const offset_t firstEntry = globalRowOffsets[destinationFirst];
+            const offset_t entryCount = globalRowOffsets[destinationLast] - firstEntry;
+            const size_t rowOffsetCount = static_cast<size_t>(destinationLast - destinationFirst) + 1;
+
+            if (destination == 0) {
+                local.values.assign(globalValues.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry),
+                                    globalValues.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry + entryCount));
+                local.columns.assign(globalColumns.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry),
+                                     globalColumns.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry + entryCount));
+                local.rowOffsets.assign(
+                    globalRowOffsets.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(destinationFirst),
+                    globalRowOffsets.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(destinationLast) + 1);
+            } else {
+                mpiSendLarge(globalRowOffsets.data() + destinationFirst, rowOffsetCount, MPI_UINT64_T,
+                             destination, 100);
+                if (entryCount != 0) {
+                    mpiSendLarge(globalValues.data() + firstEntry, static_cast<size_t>(entryCount), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                                 destination, 101);
+                    mpiSendLarge(globalColumns.data() + firstEntry, static_cast<size_t>(entryCount), MPI_UINT32_T,
+                                 destination, 102);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        local.rowOffsets.resize(static_cast<size_t>(local.rows) + 1);
+        mpiReceiveLarge(local.rowOffsets.data(), local.rowOffsets.size(), MPI_UINT64_T, 0, 100);
+        const offset_t entryCount = local.rowOffsets.back() - local.rowOffsets.front();
+        local.values.resize(static_cast<size_t>(entryCount));
+        local.columns.resize(static_cast<size_t>(entryCount));
+        mpiReceiveLarge(local.values.data(), local.values.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 101);
+        mpiReceiveLarge(local.columns.data(), local.columns.size(), MPI_UINT32_T, 0, 102);
+    }
+
+    mpiBroadcastLarge(vector.data(), vector.size(), MPI_DOUBLE);
+
+    const offset_t firstEntry = local.rowOffsets.front();
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int64_t row = 0; row < static_cast<int64_t>(local.rowOffsets.size()); ++row) {
+        local.rowOffsets[static_cast<size_t>(row)] -= firstEntry;
+    }
+    return local;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+T* allocateDevice(const size_t count) {
+    T* pointer = nullptr;
+    const size_t allocationCount = std::max<size_t>(count, 1);
+    if (allocationCount > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+        abortWithCuda(cudaErrorMemoryAllocation, "device allocation size check", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&pointer, allocationCount * sizeof(T)));
+    return pointer;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ columns,
+                               const offset_t* __restrict__ rowOffsets,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector, double* __restrict__ output,
+                               const index_t rows) {
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const unsigned int warp = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;
+    const unsigned int row = blockIdx.x * WARPS_PER_BLOCK + warp;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const offset_t begin = rowOffsets[row];
+    const offset_t end = rowOffsets[row + 1];
+    for (offset_t entry = begin + lane; entry < end; entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += values[entry] * vector[columns[entry]];
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        output[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+int selectLocalGpu() {
+    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank, MPI_INFO_NULL,
+                                  &localCommunicator));
+    int localRank = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank));
+
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        abortWithCuda(cudaErrorNoDevice, "cudaGetDeviceCount", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+    }
+    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator));
+    return device;
+}
+

+
+int main(int argc, char** argv) {

+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int destination,
+                  const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t sent = 0;
+    while (sent < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - sent));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(data + sent, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        sent += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }

+template <typename T>
+void mpiReceiveLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int source,

+    size_t received = 0;
+    while (received < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - received));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(data + received, chunk, datatype, source, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                           MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));
+        received += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }

+    size_t broadcast = 0;
+    while (broadcast < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - broadcast));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(data + broadcast, chunk, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        broadcast += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);

+LocalMatrix distributeMatrix(const Configuration& configuration, const offset_t nonZeros,
+                             std::vector<double>& vector, std::vector<double>& reference,
+                             const int ranks) {
+    const index_t firstRow = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, g_rank, ranks);
+    const index_t lastRow = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, g_rank + 1, ranks);
+    LocalMatrix local;
+    local.rows = lastRow - firstRow;
+
+    if (g_rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<double> globalValues(static_cast<size_t>(nonZeros));
+        std::vector<index_t> globalColumns(static_cast<size_t>(nonZeros));
+        std::vector<offset_t> globalRowOffsets(static_cast<size_t>(configuration.rows) + 1);
+
+        fill(vector.data(), configuration.rows, configuration.maxValue);
+        fill(globalValues.data(), nonZeros, configuration.maxValue);
+        initRandomMatrix(globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(), nonZeros, configuration.rows);
+
+        if (configuration.validate != 0) {
+            reference.resize(configuration.rows);
+            spmvCpuOpenMP(globalValues.data(), globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(),
+                           vector.data(), configuration.rows, reference.data());
+        }
+
+        for (int destination = 0; destination < ranks; ++destination) {
+            const index_t destinationFirst = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination, ranks);
+            const index_t destinationLast = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination + 1, ranks);
+            const offset_t firstEntry = globalRowOffsets[destinationFirst];
+            const offset_t entryCount = globalRowOffsets[destinationLast] - firstEntry;
+            const size_t rowOffsetCount = static_cast<size_t>(destinationLast - destinationFirst) + 1;
+
+            if (destination == 0) {
+                local.values.assign(globalValues.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry),
+                                    globalValues.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry + entryCount));
+                local.columns.assign(globalColumns.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry),
+                                     globalColumns.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry + entryCount));
+                local.rowOffsets.assign(
+                    globalRowOffsets.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(destinationFirst),
+                    globalRowOffsets.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(destinationLast) + 1);
+            } else {
+                mpiSendLarge(globalRowOffsets.data() + destinationFirst, rowOffsetCount, MPI_UINT64_T,
+                             destination, 100);
+                if (entryCount != 0) {
+                    mpiSendLarge(globalValues.data() + firstEntry, static_cast<size_t>(entryCount), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                                 destination, 101);
+                    mpiSendLarge(globalColumns.data() + firstEntry, static_cast<size_t>(entryCount), MPI_UINT32_T,
+                                 destination, 102);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        local.rowOffsets.resize(static_cast<size_t>(local.rows) + 1);
+        mpiReceiveLarge(local.rowOffsets.data(), local.rowOffsets.size(), MPI_UINT64_T, 0, 100);
+        const offset_t entryCount = local.rowOffsets.back() - local.rowOffsets.front();
+        local.values.resize(static_cast<size_t>(entryCount));
+        local.columns.resize(static_cast<size_t>(entryCount));
+        mpiReceiveLarge(local.values.data(), local.values.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 101);
+        mpiReceiveLarge(local.columns.data(), local.columns.size(), MPI_UINT32_T, 0, 102);
+    }
+
+    mpiBroadcastLarge(vector.data(), vector.size(), MPI_DOUBLE);
+
+    const offset_t firstEntry = local.rowOffsets.front();
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int64_t row = 0; row < static_cast<int64_t>(local.rowOffsets.size()); ++row) {
+        local.rowOffsets[static_cast<size_t>(row)] -= firstEntry;
+    }
+    return local;
+}

+template <typename T>
+T* allocateDevice(const size_t count) {
+    T* pointer = nullptr;
+    const size_t allocationCount = std::max<size_t>(count, 1);
+    if (allocationCount > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+        abortWithCuda(cudaErrorMemoryAllocation, "device allocation size check", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&pointer, allocationCount * sizeof(T)));
+    return pointer;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ columns,
+                               const offset_t* __restrict__ rowOffsets,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector, double* __restrict__ output,
+                               const index_t rows) {
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const unsigned int warp = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;
+    const unsigned int row = blockIdx.x * WARPS_PER_BLOCK + warp;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const offset_t begin = rowOffsets[row];
+    const offset_t end = rowOffsets[row + 1];
+    for (offset_t entry = begin + lane; entry < end; entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += values[entry] * vector[columns[entry]];
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        output[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+int selectLocalGpu() {
+    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank, MPI_INFO_NULL,
+                                  &localCommunicator));
+    int localRank = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank));
+
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        abortWithCuda(cudaErrorNoDevice, "cudaGetDeviceCount", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+    }
+    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator));
+    return device;
+}

    23	constexpr unsigned int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = 8;
    24	constexpr unsigned int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = WARP_SIZE * WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
    25	
    26	namespace {
    27	
    28	int g_rank = 0;
    29	
    30	[[noreturn]] void abortWithMpi(const char* operation, const char* file, const int line,
    31	                               const int error) {
    32	    char errorString[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING] = {};
    33	    int errorLength = 0;
    34	    MPI_Error_string(error, errorString, &errorLength);
    35	    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed at %s:%d: %.*s\n", g_rank, operation, file,
    36	                 line, errorLength, errorString);
    37	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, error);
    38	    std::abort();
    39	}
    40	
    41	void mpiCheck(const int error, const char* operation, const char* file, const int line) {
    42	    if (error != MPI_SUCCESS) {
    43	        abortWithMpi(operation, file, line, error);
    44	    }
    45	}
    46	
    47	[[noreturn]] void abortWithCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation, const char* file,
    48	                                const int line) {
    49	    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", g_rank, operation, file, line,
    50	                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
    51	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));
    52	    std::abort();
    53	}
    54	
    55	void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation, const char* file, const int line) {
    56	    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
    57	        abortWithCuda(error, operation, file, line);
    58	    }
    59	}
    60	
    61	#define MPI_CHECK(call) mpiCheck((call), #call, __FILE__, __LINE__)
    62	#define CUDA_CHECK(call) cudaCheck((call), #call, __FILE__, __LINE__)
    63	
    64	template <typename T>
    65	void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int destination,
    66	                  const int tag) {
    67	    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    68	    size_t sent = 0;
    69	    while (sent < count) {
    70	        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - sent));
    71	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(data + sent, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    72	        sent += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
    73	    }
    74	}
    75	
    76	template <typename T>
    77	void mpiReceiveLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int source,
    78	                     const int tag) {
    79	    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    80	    size_t received = 0;
    81	    while (received < count) {
    82	        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - received));
    83	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(data + received, chunk, datatype, source, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
    84	                           MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));
    85	        received += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
    86	    }
    87	}
    88	
    89	template <typename T>
    90	void mpiBroadcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype) {
    91	    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    92	    size_t broadcast = 0;
    93	    while (broadcast < count) {
    94	        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - broadcast));
    95	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(data + broadcast, chunk, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    96	        broadcast += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
    97	    }
    98	}
    99	
   100	index_t rowStartForRank(const index_t rows, const int rank, const int ranks) {
   101	    return static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(rows) * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank)) /
   102	                                static_cast<uint64_t>(ranks));
   103	}
   104	
   105	void fill(double* values, const offset_t count, const double maxValue) {
   106	    for (offset_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   107	        values[i] = maxValue * (std::rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
   108	    }
   109	}
   110	
   111	// This preserves the original CSR construction and its deterministic seed.
   112	void initRandomMatrix(index_t* columns, offset_t* rowOffsets, const offset_t nonZeros,
   113	                      const index_t dimension) {
   114	    offset_t assigned = 0;
   115	    const offset_t totalEntries = static_cast<offset_t>(dimension) * dimension;
   116	    const double probability = static_cast<double>(nonZeros) / static_cast<double>(totalEntries);
   117	
   118	    std::srand(8675309);
   119	    bool fillRemaining = false;
   120	    for (index_t row = 0; row < dimension; ++row) {
   121	        rowOffsets[row] = assigned;
   122	        for (index_t column = 0; column < dimension; ++column) {
   123	            const offset_t entry = static_cast<offset_t>(row) * dimension + column;
   124	            const offset_t entriesLeft = totalEntries - entry;
   125	            const offset_t needed = nonZeros - assigned;
   126	            if (entriesLeft <= needed) {
   127	                fillRemaining = true;
   128	            }
   129	            const double randomValue = static_cast<double>(std::rand()) / RAND_MAX;
   130	            if ((assigned < nonZeros && randomValue <= probability) || fillRemaining) {
   131	                columns[assigned++] = column;
   132	            }
   133	        }
   134	    }
   135	    rowOffsets[dimension] = nonZeros;
   136	}
   137	
   138	void spmvCpuOpenMP(const double* values, const index_t* columns, const offset_t* rowOffsets,
   139	                   const double* vector, const index_t rows, double* output) {
   140	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   141	    for (int64_t row = 0; row < static_cast<int64_t>(rows); ++row) {
   142	        double sum = 0.0;
   143	        for (offset_t entry = rowOffsets[row]; entry < rowOffsets[row + 1]; ++entry) {
   144	            sum += values[entry] * vector[columns[entry]];
   145	        }
   146	        output[row] = sum;
   147	    }
   148	}
   149	
   150	bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
   151	    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
   152	        const double ref = reference[i];
   153	        const double value = result[i];
   154	        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
   155	            if (std::abs(value) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
   156	                std::printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i,
   157	                            ref, value);
   158	                return false;
   159	            }
   160	        } else {
   161	            const double relativeError = std::abs((value - ref) / ref);
   162	            if (relativeError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
   163	                std::printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e "
   164	                            "(rel error: %.10e)\n",
   165	                            i, ref, value, relativeError);
   166	                return false;
   167	            }
   168	        }
   169	    }
   170	    return true;
   171	}
   172	
   173	void printUsage(const char* programName) {
   174	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", programName);
   175	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   176	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
   177	    std::printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
   178	    std::printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
   179	    std::printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
   180	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   181	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   182	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   183	}
   184	
   185	struct Configuration {
   186	    index_t rows = 1024;
   187	    index_t sparsity = 10;
   188	    index_t iterations = 10;
   189	    double maxValue = 1.0;
   190	    int validate = 0;
   191	    int printResults = 0;
   192	};
   193	
   194	enum class ParseResult : int { success = 0, help = 1, error = 2 };
   195	
   196	ParseResult parseArguments(const int argc, char** argv, Configuration& configuration) {
   197	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   198	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   199	            configuration.rows = static_cast<index_t>(std::atoi(argv[++i]));
   200	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   201	            configuration.sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(std::atoi(argv[++i]));
   202	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   203	            configuration.iterations = static_cast<index_t>(std::atoi(argv[++i]));
   204	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   205	            configuration.maxValue = std::atof(argv[++i]);
   206	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   207	            configuration.validate = 1;
   208	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   209	            configuration.printResults = 1;
   210	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   211	            return ParseResult::help;
   212	        } else {
   213	            std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   214	            return ParseResult::error;
   215	        }
   216	    }
   217	
   218	    if (configuration.rows == 0 || configuration.sparsity == 0) {
   219	        std::printf("Matrix size and sparsity must both be greater than zero.\n");
   220	        return ParseResult::error;
   221	    }
   222	    return ParseResult::success;
   223	}
   224	
   225	void broadcastConfiguration(Configuration& configuration, int& parseResult) {
   226	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&parseResult, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   227	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&configuration.rows, 1, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   228	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&configuration.sparsity, 1, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   229	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&configuration.iterations, 1, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   230	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&configuration.maxValue, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   231	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&configuration.validate, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   232	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&configuration.printResults, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   233	}
   234	
   235	struct LocalMatrix {
   236	    index_t rows = 0;
   237	    std::vector<double> values;
   238	    std::vector<index_t> columns;
   239	    std::vector<offset_t> rowOffsets;
   240	};
   241	
   242	LocalMatrix distributeMatrix(const Configuration& configuration, const offset_t nonZeros,
   243	                             std::vector<double>& vector, std::vector<double>& reference,
   244	                             const int ranks) {
   245	    const index_t firstRow = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, g_rank, ranks);
   246	    const index_t lastRow = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, g_rank + 1, ranks);
   247	    LocalMatrix local;
   248	    local.rows = lastRow - firstRow;
   249	
   250	    if (g_rank == 0) {
   251	        std::vector<double> globalValues(static_cast<size_t>(nonZeros));
   252	        std::vector<index_t> globalColumns(static_cast<size_t>(nonZeros));
   253	        std::vector<offset_t> globalRowOffsets(static_cast<size_t>(configuration.rows) + 1);
   254	
   255	        fill(vector.data(), configuration.rows, configuration.maxValue);
   256	        fill(globalValues.data(), nonZeros, configuration.maxValue);
   257	        initRandomMatrix(globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(), nonZeros, configuration.rows);
   258	
   259	        if (configuration.validate != 0) {
   260	            reference.resize(configuration.rows);
   261	            spmvCpuOpenMP(globalValues.data(), globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(),
   262	                           vector.data(), configuration.rows, reference.data());
   263	        }
   264	
   265	        for (int destination = 0; destination < ranks; ++destination) {
   266	            const index_t destinationFirst = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination, ranks);
   267	            const index_t destinationLast = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination + 1, ranks);
   268	            const offset_t firstEntry = globalRowOffsets[destinationFirst];
   269	            const offset_t entryCount = globalRowOffsets[destinationLast] - firstEntry;
   270	            const size_t rowOffsetCount = static_cast<size_t>(destinationLast - destinationFirst) + 1;
   271	
   272	            if (destination == 0) {
   273	                local.values.assign(globalValues.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry),
   274	                                    globalValues.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry + entryCount));
   275	                local.columns.assign(globalColumns.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry),
   276	                                     globalColumns.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry + entryCount));
   277	                local.rowOffsets.assign(
   278	                    globalRowOffsets.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(destinationFirst),
   279	                    globalRowOffsets.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(destinationLast) + 1);
   280	            } else {
   281	                mpiSendLarge(globalRowOffsets.data() + destinationFirst, rowOffsetCount, MPI_UINT64_T,
   282	                             destination, 100);
   283	                if (entryCount != 0) {
   284	                    mpiSendLarge(globalValues.data() + firstEntry, static_cast<size_t>(entryCount), MPI_DOUBLE,
   285	                                 destination, 101);
   286	                    mpiSendLarge(globalColumns.data() + firstEntry, static_cast<size_t>(entryCount), MPI_UINT32_T,
   287	                                 destination, 102);
   288	                }
   289	            }
   290	        }
   291	    } else {
   292	        local.rowOffsets.resize(static_cast<size_t>(local.rows) + 1);
   293	        mpiReceiveLarge(local.rowOffsets.data(), local.rowOffsets.size(), MPI_UINT64_T, 0, 100);
   294	        const offset_t entryCount = local.rowOffsets.back() - local.rowOffsets.front();
   295	        local.values.resize(static_cast<size_t>(entryCount));
   296	        local.columns.resize(static_cast<size_t>(entryCount));
   297	        mpiReceiveLarge(local.values.data(), local.values.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 101);
   298	        mpiReceiveLarge(local.columns.data(), local.columns.size(), MPI_UINT32_T, 0, 102);
   299	    }
   300	
   301	    mpiBroadcastLarge(vector.data(), vector.size(), MPI_DOUBLE);
   302	
   303	    const offset_t firstEntry = local.rowOffsets.front();
   304	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   305	    for (int64_t row = 0; row < static_cast<int64_t>(local.rowOffsets.size()); ++row) {
   306	        local.rowOffsets[static_cast<size_t>(row)] -= firstEntry;
   307	    }
   308	    return local;
   309	}
   310	
   311	template <typename T>
   312	T* allocateDevice(const size_t count) {
   313	    T* pointer = nullptr;
   314	    const size_t allocationCount = std::max<size_t>(count, 1);
   315	    if (allocationCount > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
   316	        abortWithCuda(cudaErrorMemoryAllocation, "device allocation size check", __FILE__, __LINE__);
   317	    }
   318	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&pointer, allocationCount * sizeof(T)));
   319	    return pointer;
   320	}
   321	
   322	__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
   323	                               const index_t* __restrict__ columns,
   324	                               const offset_t* __restrict__ rowOffsets,
   325	                               const double* __restrict__ vector, double* __restrict__ output,
   326	                               const index_t rows) {
   327	    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
   328	    const unsigned int warp = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;
   329	    const unsigned int row = blockIdx.x * WARPS_PER_BLOCK + warp;
   330	    if (row >= rows) {
   331	        return;
   332	    }
   333	
   334	    double sum = 0.0;
   335	    const offset_t begin = rowOffsets[row];
   336	    const offset_t end = rowOffsets[row + 1];
   337	    for (offset_t entry = begin + lane; entry < end; entry += WARP_SIZE) {
   338	        sum += values[entry] * vector[columns[entry]];
   339	    }
   340	
   341	    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
   342	        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
   343	    }
   344	    if (lane == 0) {
   345	        output[row] = sum;
   346	    }
   347	}
   348	
   349	int selectLocalGpu() {
   350	    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   351	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank, MPI_INFO_NULL,
   352	                                  &localCommunicator));
   353	    int localRank = 0;
   354	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank));
   355	
   356	    int deviceCount = 0;
   357	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
   358	    if (deviceCount == 0) {
   359	        abortWithCuda(cudaErrorNoDevice, "cudaGetDeviceCount", __FILE__, __LINE__);
   360	    }
   361	    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;
   362	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));
   363	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator));
   364	    return device;
   365	}
   366	
   367	}  // namespace
   368	
   369	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   370	    int providedThreadLevel = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
   371	    const int initResult = MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &providedThreadLevel);
   372	    if (initResult != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   373	        std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI_Init_thread failed.\n");
   374	        return 1;
   375	    }
   376	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank));
   377	    int ranks = 1;
   378	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
   379	
   380	    Configuration configuration;
   381	    int parseResult = static_cast<int>(ParseResult::success);
   382	    if (g_rank == 0) {
   383	        parseResult = static_cast<int>(parseArguments(argc, argv, configuration));
   384	        if (parseResult != static_cast<int>(ParseResult::success)) {
   385	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   386	        }
   387	    }
   388	    broadcastConfiguration(configuration, parseResult);
   389	    if (parseResult != static_cast<int>(ParseResult::success)) {
   390	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
   391	        return parseResult == static_cast<int>(ParseResult::help) ? 0 : 1;
   392	    }
   393	
   394	    const offset_t matrixEntries = static_cast<offset_t>(configuration.rows) * configuration.rows;
   395	    const offset_t nonZeros = matrixEntries / configuration.sparsity;
   396	    if (nonZeros > static_cast<offset_t>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
   397	        if (g_rank == 0) {
   398	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Matrix is too large for this platform's address space.\n");
   399	        }
   400	        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
   401	        return 1;
   402	    }
   403	
   404	    if (g_rank == 0) {
   405	        std::printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
   406	        std::printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", configuration.rows, configuration.rows);
   407	        std::printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n", configuration.sparsity);
   408	        std::printf("Non-zero elements: %llu (%.2f%% sparse)\n",
   409	                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(nonZeros),
   410	                    100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nonZeros) / matrixEntries));
   411	        std::printf("Iterations: %u\n", configuration.iterations);
   412	        std::printf("Max value: %.2f\n", configuration.maxValue);
   413	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", configuration.validate != 0 ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   414	        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", ranks);
   415	        std::printf("OpenMP threads per rank: %d\n", omp_get_max_threads());
   416	        if (providedThreadLevel < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
   417	            std::printf("MPI thread level is below MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED; MPI calls remain on the main thread.\n");
   418	        }
   419	        std::printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
   420	    }
   421	
   422	    std::vector<double> vector(configuration.rows);
   423	    std::vector<double> reference;
   424	    LocalMatrix local = distributeMatrix(configuration, nonZeros, vector, reference, ranks);
   425	
   426	    const int device = selectLocalGpu();
   427	    if (g_rank == 0) {
   428	        std::printf("Computing SpMV on CUDA GPUs (root rank uses device %d)...\n", device);
   429	    }
   430	
   431	    double* deviceValues = allocateDevice<double>(local.values.size());
   432	    index_t* deviceColumns = allocateDevice<index_t>(local.columns.size());
   433	    offset_t* deviceRowOffsets = allocateDevice<offset_t>(local.rowOffsets.size());
   434	    double* deviceVector = allocateDevice<double>(vector.size());
   435	    double* deviceOutput = allocateDevice<double>(local.rows);
   436	
   437	    if (!local.values.empty()) {
   438	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceValues, local.values.data(), local.values.size() * sizeof(double),
   439	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   440	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceColumns, local.columns.data(), local.columns.size() * sizeof(index_t),
   441	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   442	    }
   443	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceRowOffsets, local.rowOffsets.data(),
   444	                          local.rowOffsets.size() * sizeof(offset_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   445	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceVector, vector.data(), vector.size() * sizeof(double),
   446	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   447	    // Match the original value-initialized host output when the iteration count is zero.
   448	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(deviceOutput, 0, std::max<size_t>(local.rows, 1) * sizeof(double)));
   449	
   450	    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
   451	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   452	    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
   453	    cudaGraphExec_t graphExecution = nullptr;
   454	    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
   455	    cudaEvent_t endEvent = nullptr;
   456	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
   457	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&endEvent));
   458	
   459	    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
   460	        (static_cast<uint64_t>(local.rows) + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK);
   461	    if (local.rows != 0) {
   462	        // Capturing the fixed CSR launch avoids per-iteration host launch overhead.
   463	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeGlobal));
   464	        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(
   465	            deviceValues, deviceColumns, deviceRowOffsets, deviceVector, deviceOutput, local.rows);
   466	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   467	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
   468	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graphExecution, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
   469	    }
   470	
   471	    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
   472	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent, stream));
   473	    for (index_t iteration = 0; iteration < configuration.iterations; ++iteration) {
   474	        if (local.rows != 0) {
   475	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graphExecution, stream));
   476	        }
   477	    }
   478	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(endEvent, stream));

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithMpi(const char* operation, const char* file, const int line,

+    int errorLength = 0;

+    std::abort();

+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int destination,
+                  const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t sent = 0;
+    while (sent < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - sent));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(data + sent, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        sent += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiReceiveLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int source,
+                     const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t received = 0;
+    while (received < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - received));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(data + received, chunk, datatype, source, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                           MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));
+        received += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBroadcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t broadcast = 0;
+    while (broadcast < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - broadcast));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(data + broadcast, chunk, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        broadcast += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}

+            reference.resize(configuration.rows);
+            spmvCpuOpenMP(globalValues.data(), globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(),
+                           vector.data(), configuration.rows, reference.data());
+        }
+
+        for (int destination = 0; destination < ranks; ++destination) {
+            const index_t destinationFirst = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination, ranks);
+            const index_t destinationLast = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination + 1, ranks);
+            const offset_t firstEntry = globalRowOffsets[destinationFirst];
+            const offset_t entryCount = globalRowOffsets[destinationLast] - firstEntry;
+            const size_t rowOffsetCount = static_cast<size_t>(destinationLast - destinationFirst) + 1;
+
+            if (destination == 0) {
+                local.values.assign(globalValues.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry),
+                                    globalValues.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry + entryCount));
+                local.columns.assign(globalColumns.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry),
+                                     globalColumns.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry + entryCount));
+                local.rowOffsets.assign(
+                    globalRowOffsets.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(destinationFirst),
+                    globalRowOffsets.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(destinationLast) + 1);
+            } else {
+                mpiSendLarge(globalRowOffsets.data() + destinationFirst, rowOffsetCount, MPI_UINT64_T,
+                             destination, 100);
+                if (entryCount != 0) {
+                    mpiSendLarge(globalValues.data() + firstEntry, static_cast<size_t>(entryCount), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                                 destination, 101);
+                    mpiSendLarge(globalColumns.data() + firstEntry, static_cast<size_t>(entryCount), MPI_UINT32_T,
+                                 destination, 102);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        local.rowOffsets.resize(static_cast<size_t>(local.rows) + 1);
+        mpiReceiveLarge(local.rowOffsets.data(), local.rowOffsets.size(), MPI_UINT64_T, 0, 100);
+        const offset_t entryCount = local.rowOffsets.back() - local.rowOffsets.front();
+        local.values.resize(static_cast<size_t>(entryCount));
+        local.columns.resize(static_cast<size_t>(entryCount));
+        mpiReceiveLarge(local.values.data(), local.values.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 101);
+        mpiReceiveLarge(local.columns.data(), local.columns.size(), MPI_UINT32_T, 0, 102);
+    }
+
+    mpiBroadcastLarge(vector.data(), vector.size(), MPI_DOUBLE);
+
+    const offset_t firstEntry = local.rowOffsets.front();
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int64_t row = 0; row < static_cast<int64_t>(local.rowOffsets.size()); ++row) {
+        local.rowOffsets[static_cast<size_t>(row)] -= firstEntry;
+    }
+    return local;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+T* allocateDevice(const size_t count) {
+    T* pointer = nullptr;
+    const size_t allocationCount = std::max<size_t>(count, 1);
+    if (allocationCount > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+        abortWithCuda(cudaErrorMemoryAllocation, "device allocation size check", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&pointer, allocationCount * sizeof(T)));
+    return pointer;
+}
+
+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ columns,
+                               const offset_t* __restrict__ rowOffsets,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector, double* __restrict__ output,
+                               const index_t rows) {
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const unsigned int warp = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;
+    const unsigned int row = blockIdx.x * WARPS_PER_BLOCK + warp;
+    if (row >= rows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    const offset_t begin = rowOffsets[row];
+    const offset_t end = rowOffsets[row + 1];
+    for (offset_t entry = begin + lane; entry < end; entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += values[entry] * vector[columns[entry]];
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        output[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
+int selectLocalGpu() {
+    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank, MPI_INFO_NULL,
+                                  &localCommunicator));
+    int localRank = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank));
+
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        abortWithCuda(cudaErrorNoDevice, "cudaGetDeviceCount", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+    }
+    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator));
+    return device;
+}
+
+}  // namespace

+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int destination,
+                  const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t sent = 0;
+    while (sent < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - sent));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(data + sent, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        sent += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiReceiveLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int source,
+                     const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t received = 0;
+    while (received < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - received));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(data + received, chunk, datatype, source, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                           MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));
+        received += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBroadcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t broadcast = 0;
+    while (broadcast < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - broadcast));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(data + broadcast, chunk, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        broadcast += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}

+    return static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(rows) * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank)) /
+                                static_cast<uint64_t>(ranks));
+}

+T* allocateDevice(const size_t count) {
+    T* pointer = nullptr;
+    const size_t allocationCount = std::max<size_t>(count, 1);
+    if (allocationCount > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {

+    }

+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ columns,
+                               const offset_t* __restrict__ rowOffsets,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector, double* __restrict__ output,
+                               const index_t rows) {
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const unsigned int warp = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;

+    const offset_t end = rowOffsets[row + 1];
+    for (offset_t entry = begin + lane; entry < end; entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += values[entry] * vector[columns[entry]];
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        output[row] = sum;

+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;

+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;

+    if (local.rows != 0) {

+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeGlobal));
+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(
+            deviceValues, deviceColumns, deviceRowOffsets, deviceVector, deviceOutput, local.rows);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graphExecution, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    }
+
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent, stream));
+    for (index_t iteration = 0; iteration < configuration.iterations; ++iteration) {

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&localMilliseconds, startEvent, endEvent));
+    double elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0;
+    const double localElapsedMilliseconds = static_cast<double>(localMilliseconds);
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&localElapsedMilliseconds, &elapsedMilliseconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+
+    const bool needHostOutput = configuration.validate != 0 || configuration.printResults != 0;
+    std::vector<double> localOutput;
+    std::vector<double> output;
+    if (needHostOutput) {
+        localOutput.resize(local.rows);
+        if (!localOutput.empty()) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(localOutput.data(), deviceOutput, localOutput.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        }
 
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
+        if (g_rank == 0) {
+            output.resize(configuration.rows);
+            std::copy(localOutput.begin(), localOutput.end(), output.begin());
+            for (int source = 1; source < ranks; ++source) {
+                const index_t sourceFirst = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, source, ranks);
+                const index_t sourceLast = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, source + 1, ranks);
+                mpiReceiveLarge(output.data() + sourceFirst, static_cast<size_t>(sourceLast - sourceFirst),
+                                MPI_DOUBLE, source, 103);
+            }
         } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+            mpiSendLarge(localOutput.data(), localOutput.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 103);
+        }
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(endEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
+    if (graphExecution != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graphExecution));
+    }
+    if (graph != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceOutput));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceVector));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceRowOffsets));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceColumns));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceValues));
+
+    int exitCode = 0;
+    if (g_rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", elapsedMilliseconds);
+        const double averageTime = configuration.iterations == 0
+                                       ? 0.0
+                                       : elapsedMilliseconds / configuration.iterations;
+        const double gflops = elapsedMilliseconds == 0.0
+                                  ? 0.0
+                                  : (2.0 * static_cast<double>(nonZeros) * configuration.iterations) /
+                                        (elapsedMilliseconds / 1000.0) / 1.0e9;
+        std::printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", averageTime);
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (configuration.printResults != 0) {
+            print_results(output, "OutputVector");
         }
+        if (configuration.validate != 0) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (verifyResults(reference.data(), output.data(), configuration.rows)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                exitCode = 1;
+            }
+        }
     }
 
-    return 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
+    return exitCode;
 }

+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int destination,
+                  const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t sent = 0;
+    while (sent < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - sent));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(data + sent, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        sent += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiReceiveLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int source,
+                     const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t received = 0;
+    while (received < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - received));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(data + received, chunk, datatype, source, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                           MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));
+        received += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBroadcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t broadcast = 0;
+    while (broadcast < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - broadcast));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(data + broadcast, chunk, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        broadcast += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}

+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&configuration.maxValue, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&configuration.validate, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));

+struct LocalMatrix {
+    index_t rows = 0;
+    std::vector<double> values;
+    std::vector<index_t> columns;
+    std::vector<offset_t> rowOffsets;
+};
+
+LocalMatrix distributeMatrix(const Configuration& configuration, const offset_t nonZeros,
+                             std::vector<double>& vector, std::vector<double>& reference,
+                             const int ranks) {
+    const index_t firstRow = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, g_rank, ranks);
+    const index_t lastRow = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, g_rank + 1, ranks);
+    LocalMatrix local;
+    local.rows = lastRow - firstRow;
+
+    if (g_rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<double> globalValues(static_cast<size_t>(nonZeros));
+        std::vector<index_t> globalColumns(static_cast<size_t>(nonZeros));
+        std::vector<offset_t> globalRowOffsets(static_cast<size_t>(configuration.rows) + 1);
+
+        fill(vector.data(), configuration.rows, configuration.maxValue);
+        fill(globalValues.data(), nonZeros, configuration.maxValue);
+        initRandomMatrix(globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(), nonZeros, configuration.rows);
+
+        if (configuration.validate != 0) {
+            reference.resize(configuration.rows);
+            spmvCpuOpenMP(globalValues.data(), globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(),
+                           vector.data(), configuration.rows, reference.data());
         }
+
+        for (int destination = 0; destination < ranks; ++destination) {
+            const index_t destinationFirst = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination, ranks);
+            const index_t destinationLast = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination + 1, ranks);
+            const offset_t firstEntry = globalRowOffsets[destinationFirst];
+            const offset_t entryCount = globalRowOffsets[destinationLast] - firstEntry;
+            const size_t rowOffsetCount = static_cast<size_t>(destinationLast - destinationFirst) + 1;
+
+            if (destination == 0) {
+                local.values.assign(globalValues.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry),
+                                    globalValues.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry + entryCount));
+                local.columns.assign(globalColumns.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry),
+                                     globalColumns.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(firstEntry + entryCount));
+                local.rowOffsets.assign(
+                    globalRowOffsets.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(destinationFirst),
+                    globalRowOffsets.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(destinationLast) + 1);
+            } else {
+                mpiSendLarge(globalRowOffsets.data() + destinationFirst, rowOffsetCount, MPI_UINT64_T,
+                             destination, 100);
+                if (entryCount != 0) {
+                    mpiSendLarge(globalValues.data() + firstEntry, static_cast<size_t>(entryCount), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                                 destination, 101);
+                    mpiSendLarge(globalColumns.data() + firstEntry, static_cast<size_t>(entryCount), MPI_UINT32_T,
+                                 destination, 102);
+                }
+            }

+T* allocateDevice(const size_t count) {

+                               const index_t* __restrict__ columns,
+                               const offset_t* __restrict__ rowOffsets,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector, double* __restrict__ output,

+        sum += values[entry] * vector[columns[entry]];
+    }

+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }

+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int destination,
+                  const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t sent = 0;
+    while (sent < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - sent));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(data + sent, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        sent += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiReceiveLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int source,
+                     const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t received = 0;
+    while (received < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - received));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(data + received, chunk, datatype, source, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                           MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));
+        received += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBroadcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t broadcast = 0;
+    while (broadcast < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - broadcast));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(data + broadcast, chunk, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        broadcast += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+index_t rowStartForRank(const index_t rows, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    return static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(rows) * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank)) /
+                                static_cast<uint64_t>(ranks));
+}
+
+void fill(double* values, const offset_t count, const double maxValue) {
+    for (offset_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+        values[i] = maxValue * (std::rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
+    }
+}
+

+T* allocateDevice(const size_t count) {

+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ columns,
+                               const offset_t* __restrict__ rowOffsets,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector, double* __restrict__ output,
+                               const index_t rows) {
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const unsigned int warp = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;

+    const offset_t end = rowOffsets[row + 1];
+    for (offset_t entry = begin + lane; entry < end; entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += values[entry] * vector[columns[entry]];
+    }
+

+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        output[row] = sum;

+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;

+        spmvWarpKernel<<<blocks, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(

+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));

+    }
+
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent, stream));
+    for (index_t iteration = 0; iteration < configuration.iterations; ++iteration) {

Implemented the fixed MPI + OpenMP + CUDA hybrid SpMV benchmark.

- [spmv.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_hybrid_r1/spmv/spmv.cpp:242) partitions contiguous CSR rows across MPI ranks, selects a node-local GPU per rank, runs a warp-per-row CUDA kernel via CUDA Graph replay, and uses OpenMP for CSR preparation/reference validation.
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_hybrid_r1/spmv/CMakeLists.txt:1) now requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA; the executable remains `spmv`.

Validated successfully with CUDA hardware using 1–4 MPI ranks, including validation/output and empty-row partitions.

+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int destination,
+                  const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t sent = 0;
+    while (sent < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - sent));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(data + sent, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        sent += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiReceiveLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int source,
+                     const int tag) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t received = 0;
+    while (received < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - received));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(data + received, chunk, datatype, source, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                           MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));
+        received += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBroadcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, const MPI_Datatype datatype) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t broadcast = 0;
+    while (broadcast < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, count - broadcast));
+        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(data + broadcast, chunk, datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+        broadcast += static_cast<size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}

+LocalMatrix distributeMatrix(const Configuration& configuration, const offset_t nonZeros,
+                             std::vector<double>& vector, std::vector<double>& reference,
+                             const int ranks) {
+    const index_t firstRow = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, g_rank, ranks);
+    const index_t lastRow = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, g_rank + 1, ranks);
+    LocalMatrix local;
+    local.rows = lastRow - firstRow;
+
+    if (g_rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<double> globalValues(static_cast<size_t>(nonZeros));
+        std::vector<index_t> globalColumns(static_cast<size_t>(nonZeros));
+        std::vector<offset_t> globalRowOffsets(static_cast<size_t>(configuration.rows) + 1);
+
+        fill(vector.data(), configuration.rows, configuration.maxValue);
+        fill(globalValues.data(), nonZeros, configuration.maxValue);
+        initRandomMatrix(globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(), nonZeros, configuration.rows);
+
+        if (configuration.validate != 0) {
+            reference.resize(configuration.rows);
+            spmvCpuOpenMP(globalValues.data(), globalColumns.data(), globalRowOffsets.data(),
+                           vector.data(), configuration.rows, reference.data());
         }
+
+        for (int destination = 0; destination < ranks; ++destination) {
+            const index_t destinationFirst = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination, ranks);
+            const index_t destinationLast = rowStartForRank(configuration.rows, destination + 1, ranks);
+            const offset_t firstEntry = globalRowOffsets[destinationFirst];
+            const offset_t entryCount = globalRowOffsets[destinationLast] - firstEntry;
+            const size_t rowOffsetCount = static_cast<size_t>(destinationLast - destinationFirst) + 1;
+

+__global__ void spmvWarpKernel(const double* __restrict__ values,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ columns,
+                               const offset_t* __restrict__ rowOffsets,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vector, double* __restrict__ output,
+                               const index_t rows) {

+    const offset_t begin = rowOffsets[row];
+    const offset_t end = rowOffsets[row + 1];
+    for (offset_t entry = begin + lane; entry < end; entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum += values[entry] * vector[columns[entry]];
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+    if (lane == 0) {

Implemented the fixed MPI + OpenMP + CUDA hybrid SpMV benchmark.

- [spmv.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_hybrid_r1/spmv/spmv.cpp:242) partitions contiguous CSR rows across MPI ranks, selects a node-local GPU per rank, runs a warp-per-row CUDA kernel via CUDA Graph replay, and uses OpenMP for CSR preparation/reference validation.
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_hybrid_r1/spmv/CMakeLists.txt:1) now requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA; the executable remains `spmv`.

Validated successfully with CUDA hardware using 1–4 MPI ranks, including validation/output and empty-row partitions.
